178→            &ctx->current_epoch.directors[ctx->current_epoch.num_directors++];\n   179→        memcpy(d->pubkey, rec->pubkey, sizeof(d->pubkey));\n   180→        d->stake         = rec->stake;\n 204→static void\n   205→da_free(consensus_strategy_t *iface)\n   206→{\n   207→    if (!iface || !iface->impl_ctx) return;\n   208→\n   209→    da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx = iface->impl_ctx;\n   210→\n   211→    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&ctx->rwlock);\n   212→    free(ctx);\n   213→    iface->impl_ctx = NULL;\n   214→}\n   215→\n   216→/*\n   217→ * validate_block:\n   218→ *   1. Validate tx root & state transition via engine API.\n   221→ */\n   222→static bool\n   223→da_validate_block(consensus_strategy_t *iface,\n   224→                  const block_t        *blk,\n   225→                  char                  err_buf[CONS_STRAT_ERRBUF_SZ])\n   226→{\n   227→    if (!iface || !blk) return false;\n   228→\n   229→    da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx = iface->impl_ctx;\n   230→ 239→        goto exit;\n   240→    }\n   241→\n   242→    if (memcmp(expected_leader.pubkey,\n   243→               blk->header.proposer_pubkey,\n   244→               sizeof(expected_leader.pubkey)) blk->header.proposer_sig,\n   255→                            expected_leader.pubkey))\n   256→    {\n   257→        snprintf(err_buf, CONS_STRAT_ERRBUF_SZ,\n   258→                 \"Invalid leader 266→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n   267→    return ok;\n   268→}\n   269→\n   270→/*\n   271→ * apply_block:\n   272→ *   Mutates strategy-specific state after the block has been This is the place to apply rewards,\n   274→ *   re-calc epochs, slash misbehaviour, etc.\n   275→ */\n   276→static bool\n   277→da_apply_block(consensus_strategy_t *iface, const block_t *blk)\n   278→{\n   279→    if (!iface || !blk) return false;\n   280→\n   281→    da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx = iface->impl_ctx;\n   282→    bool               ok  = true;\n   283→\n   284→ leader.pubkey,\n   297→                                      leader.stake,\n   298→                                      leader.missed_blocks,\n   299→                                      leader.jailed);\n   300→    }\n   301→\n   302→    /* Reward distribution */\n   303→    uint64_t reward = da_calc_block_reward(ctx, &leader);\n   304→    if (reward > 0) {\n   305→        /* Mint   if (!da_recalc_epoch_if_needed(ctx, new_height)) {\n   311→        ok = false;\n   312→    }\n   313→\n   314→    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n   315→    return ok;\n   316→}\n   317→\n   318→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n   319→ * Helper Implementations\n   320→ * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   321→\n   322→static bool\n   323→da_recalc_epoch_if_needed(da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx, uint64_t new_height)\n   324→{\n   325→    if ((new_height % DA_EPOCH_LENGTH) != 0) {\n   326→        return true; /* Nothing to do */\n   327→    }\n   328→\n   329→    uint64_t next_epoch_index = ctx->current_epoch.epoch_index + 1;\n    {\n   342→        const director_record_t *rec = state_director_iter_record(&it);\n   343→\n   344→        if (rec->stake < DA_MIN_STAKE || rec->jailed) {\n   345→            continue;\n   346→ += rec->stake;\n   359→    }\n   360→    state_director_iter_close(&it);\n   361→\n   362→    /* If no directors, strategy cannot continue */\n   363→    if (next.num_directors == 0) {\n   364→        LOG_CRITICAL(\"DA_STRATEGY: No eligible directors for epoch %\" PRIu64,\n   365→                     next_epoch_index);\n   366→        return false;\n   367→    }\n   368→\n   369→    /* Sort ctx->current_epoch = next;\n   376→    da_log_director_set(&ctx->current_epoch);\n   377→\n   378→    /* Emit event for observability */\n   379→    event_bus_publish(ctx->bus, EVENT_EPOCH_CHANGED, &next, sizeof(next));\n   380→\n   381→    return true;\n   382→}\n   383→\n   384→/*\n   385→ * Deterministic leader selection using weighted-round-robin proportional to\n   386→ * stake, salted by epoch index to prevent grinding.\n   387→ */\n   388→static bool\n   389→da_select_leader(const da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx,\n   390→                 uint64_t                 height,\n   391→                 da_director_t           *out)\n   392→{\n   393→    if (ctx->current_epoch.num_directors == 0) {\n   394→        return false;\n   395→    }\n   396→\n   397→    uint64_t slot = height % ctx->current_epoch.num_directors;\n   398→\n   399→    /* SHA3(epoch||height) to produce pseudo-random permutation */\n   400→    uint8_t seed_hash[SHA3_256_DIGEST_LEN];\n   401→    uint8_t input[sizeof(uint64_t) * 2];\n   402→    memcpy(input, &ctx->current_epoch.epoch_index, sizeof(uint64_t));\n   403→    memcpy(input + sizeof(uint64_t), &height, sizeof(uint64_t));\n   404→    sha3_256(seed_hash, sizeof(seed_hash), input, sizeof(input));\n   405→\n   406→    uint64_t rnd;\n   407→    memcpy(&rnd, seed_hash, sizeof(rnd));\n   408→    rnd %= true;\n   414→}\n   415→\n   416→static uint64_t\n   417→da_calc_block_reward(const da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx,\n   418→                     const da_director_t     *leader)\n   419→{\n   420→    if (ctx->cfg.fixed_reward > 0) {\n   421→        return ctx->cfg.fixed_reward;\n   422→    }\n   423→\n   424→    /* Otherwise reward proportional to stake */\n   425→    double pct = (double)DA_REWARD_BASE_PERCENT / 100.0;\n   426→    double reward = (double)leader->stake * pct;\n   427→    return (uint64_t)reward;\n   428→}\n   429→\n   430→/*\n   431→ * Slash a director by percentage of stake, jail if severe.\n   432→ */\n   433→static void\n   434→da_apply_slash(da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx,\n   435→               da_director_t     *offender,\n   436→               uint32_t offender->pubkey,\n   456→                                  offender->stake,\n   457→                                  offender->missed_blocks,\n   458→     \" with %zu directors (Σ stake=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n   466→             epoch->epoch_index,\n   467→             epoch->num_directors,\n   468→             epoch->total_stake);\n   469→\n   470→#ifdef DEBUG\n   471→    for (size_t i = 0; i < epoch->num_directors; ++i) {\n   472→        char hex[ED25519_PUBKEY_LEN * 2 + 1];\n   473→        bytes_to_hex(epoch->directors[i].pubkey,\n   474→                     sizeof(epoch->directors[i].pubkey),\n   475→                     hex,\n   476→                     sizeof(hex));\n   477→        LOG_DEBUG(\"DA_STRATEGY: Director[%zu]: pk=%s (da->stake > db->stake) return -1;\n   491→    if (da->stake < db->stake) return  1;\n   492→    return memcmp(da->pubkey, db->pubkey, sizeof(da->pubkey));\n   493→}\n   494→\n   495→/* DA is a delegated Proof-of-Stake variant\n *   optimised for collaborative, generative-art workloads.  Curators delegate\n *   “inspiration weight” to trusted validator nodes (“Art Directors”),\n *   incentivising rapid block finality while preserving decentralisation by\n *   periodically rotating directors via on-chain votes.\n *\n *   The implementation follows the Strategy Pattern.  The generic consensus\n *   engine (consensus_engine.c) calls the function pointers exposed in\n *   consensus_strategy_t.  Swapping between DA and alternative strategies\n *   (e.g., PoS-Staking) therefore requires no changes in the core engine.\n *\n *   NOTE: This module purposefully avoids direct networking / I/O. bool\nda_init(consensus_strategy_t *iface,\n        consensus_engine_t    *engine,\n        const void            *params,\n        size_t                 params_len)\n{\n    if (!iface || !engine) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    (void)params_len; /* Unused until we version-tag params */\n\n    const da_config_t *cfg = (const da_config_t *)params;\n    if (!cfg) {\n       d->missed_blocks = 0;\n        d->jailed        = false;\n        ctx->current_epoch.total_stake += rec->stake;\n    }\n\n    state_director_iter_close(&it);\n\n    /* Sort directors pthread_rwlock_destroy(&ctx->rwlock);\n    free(ctx);\n    iface->impl_ctx = NULL;\n}\n\n/*\n * validate_block:\n *   1. Enforce correct leader for height.\n *   2. Verify signature.\n *   3. Validate tx root & state transition via engine API.\n */\nstatic bool\nda_validate_block(consensus_strategy_t *iface,\n                  const block_t        *blk,\n                  char                  err_buf[CONS_STRAT_ERRBUF_SZ])\n{\n    if (!iface || !blk) return false;\n\n    da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx = iface->impl_ctx;\n    bool ok = false;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n\n    da_director_t expected_leader;\n    if (!da_select_leader(ctx, blk->header.height, &expected_leader)) {\n        snprintf(err_buf, CONS_STRAT_ERRBUF_SZ,\n                 \"Failed to select leader for height %\" PRIu64,\n                 blk->header.height);\n        goto exit;\n    }\n\n    if (memcmp(expected_leader.pubkey,\n               blk->header.proposer_pubkey,\n               sizeof(expected_leader.pubkey)) != 0)\n    {\n        snprintf(err_buf, CONS_STRAT_ERRBUF_SZ,\n                 \"Block proposer not expected leader\");\n        goto exit;\n    }\n\n    /* Verify proposer signature */\n    if (!sig_verify_ed25519(blk->header_hash,\n                            sizeof(blk->header_hash),\n                            blk->header.proposer_sig,\n                            expected_leader.pubkey))\n    {\n        snprintf(err_buf, CONS_STRAT_ERRBUF_SZ,\n                 \"Invalid leader signature\");\n        goto exit;\n    }\n\n    /* Additional validation deferred to engine (tx Merkle, receipts, etc.) */\n    ok = true;\n\nexit:\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/*\n * apply_block:\n *   Mutates strategy-specific state after the block has been committed to the\n *   canonical chain (height → height+1).  This is the place to apply rewards,\n *   re-calc epochs, slash misbehaviour, etc.\n */\nstatic bool\nda_apply_block(consensus_strategy_t *iface, const block_t *blk)\n{\n    if (!iface || !blk) return false;\n\n    da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx = iface->impl_ctx;\n    bool               ok  = true;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n\n    uint64_t new_height = blk->header.height;\n    atomic_store(&ctx->height, new_height);\n\n    /* Update last_signed for leader */\n    da_director_t leader;\n    if (da_select_leader(ctx, new_height, &leader)) {\n        leader.last_signed = new_height;\n        leader.missed_blocks = 0; /* Reset missed block counter */\n        /* Persist update to world-state asynchronously */\n        state_enqueue_director_update(ctx->state,\n                                      leader.pubkey,\n                                      leader.stake,\n                                      leader.missed_blocks,\n                                      leader.jailed);\n    }\n\n    /* Reward distribution */\n    uint64_t reward = da_calc_block_reward(ctx, &leader);\n    if (reward > 0) {\n        /* Mint reward to leader’s account; engine handles supply inflation */\n        state_credit_account(ctx->state, leader.pubkey, reward);\n    }\n\n    /* Epoch change? */\n    if (!da_recalc_epoch_if_needed(ctx, new_height)) {\n        ok = false;\n    }\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Helper Implementations\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic bool\nda_recalc_epoch_if_needed(da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx, uint64_t new_height)\n{\n    if ((new_height % DA_EPOCH_LENGTH) != 0) {\n        return true; /* Nothing to do */\n    }\n\n    uint64_t next_epoch_index = ctx->current_epoch.epoch_index + 1;\n    da_epoch_t next = {\n        .num_directors = 0,\n        .total_stake   = 0,\n        .epoch_index   = next_epoch_index\n    };\n\n    /* Collect candidate directors from world-state */\n    state_director_iter_t it;\n    state_director_iter_init(ctx->state, &it);\n\n    while (state_director_iter_next(&it))\n    {\n        const director_record_t *rec = state_director_iter_record(&it);\n\n        if (rec->stake < DA_MIN_STAKE || rec->jailed) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if (next.num_directors >= DA_MAX_DIRECTORS) {\n            LOG_WARN(\"DA_STRATEGY: Director set truncated to %u\", DA_MAX_DIRECTORS);\n            break;\n        }\n\n        da_director_t *d = &next.directors[next.num_directors++];\n        memcpy(d->pubkey, rec->pubkey, sizeof(d->pubkey));\n        d->stake         = rec->stake;\n        d->last_signed   = rec->last_signed;\n        d->missed_blocks = rec->missed_blocks;\n        d->jailed        = rec->jailed;\n        next.total_stake += rec->stake;\n    }\n    state_director_iter_close(&it);\n\n    /* If no directors, strategy cannot continue */\n    if (next.num_directors == 0) {\n        LOG_CRITICAL(\"DA_STRATEGY: No eligible directors for epoch %\" PRIu64,\n                     next_epoch_index);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Sort deterministically */\n    qsort(next.directors,\n          next.num_directors,\n          sizeof(da_director_t),\n          da_compare_director);\n\n    ctx->current_epoch = next;\n    da_log_director_set(&ctx->current_epoch);\n\n    /* Emit event for observability */\n    event_bus_publish(ctx->bus, EVENT_EPOCH_CHANGED, &next, sizeof(next));\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n/*\n * Deterministic leader selection using weighted-round-robin proportional to\n * stake, salted by epoch index to prevent grinding.\n */\nstatic bool\nda_select_leader(const da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx,\n                 uint64_t                 height,\n                 da_director_t           *out)\n{\n    if (ctx->current_epoch.num_directors == 0) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    uint64_t slot = height % ctx->current_epoch.num_directors;\n\n    /* SHA3(epoch||height) to produce pseudo-random permutation */\n    uint8_t seed_hash[SHA3_256_DIGEST_LEN];\n    uint8_t input[sizeof(uint64_t) * 2];\n    memcpy(input, &ctx->current_epoch.epoch_index, sizeof(uint64_t));\n    memcpy(input + sizeof(uint64_t), &height, sizeof(uint64_t));\n    sha3_256(seed_hash, sizeof(seed_hash), input, sizeof(input));\n\n    uint64_t rnd;\n    memcpy(&rnd, seed_hash, sizeof(rnd));\n    rnd %= ctx->current_epoch.num_directors;\n\n    size_t idx = (slot + rnd) % ctx->current_epoch.num_directors;\n\n    *out = ctx->current_epoch.directors[idx];\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic uint64_t\nda_calc_block_reward(const da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx,\n                     const da_director_t     *leader)\n{\n    if (ctx->cfg.fixed_reward > 0) {\n        return ctx->cfg.fixed_reward;\n    }\n\n    /* Otherwise reward proportional to stake */\n    double pct = (double)DA_REWARD_BASE_PERCENT / 100.0;\n    double reward = (double)leader->stake * pct;\n    return (uint64_t)reward;\n}\n\n/*\n * Slash a director by percentage of stake, jail if severe.\n */\nstatic void\nda_apply_slash(da_strategy_ctx_t *ctx,\n               da_director_t     *offender,\n               uint32_t           percent)\n{\n    if (percent == 0 || percent > DA_MAX_SLASH_PERCENT) return;\n\n    uint64_t penalty = (offender->stake * percent) / 100;\n    offender->stake -= penalty;\n\n    state_debit_account(ctx->state, offender->pubkey, penalty);\n\n    LOG_WARN(\"DA_STRATEGY: Director slashed %\" PRIu32 \"%% (penalty=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n             percent, penalty);\n\n    if (percent == DA_MAX_SLASH_PERCENT) {\n        offender->jailed = true;\n        LOG_WARN(\"DA_STRATEGY: Director jailed due to severe slash\");\n    }\n\n    /* Persist director state */\n    state_enqueue_director_update(ctx->state,\n                                  offender->pubkey,\n                                  offender->stake,\n                                  offender->missed_blocks,\n                                  offender->jailed);\n}\n\nstatic void\nda_log_director_set(const da_epoch_t *epoch)\n{\n    LOG_INFO(\"DA_STRATEGY: Activated epoch %\" PRIu64\n             \" with %zu directors (Σ stake=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n             epoch->epoch_index,\n             epoch->num_directors,\n             epoch->total_stake);\n\n#ifdef DEBUG\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < epoch->num_directors; ++i) {\n        char hex[ED25519_PUBKEY_LEN * 2 + 1];\n        bytes_to_hex(epoch->directors[i].pubkey,\n                     sizeof(epoch->directors[i].pubkey),\n                     hex,\n                     sizeof(hex));\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"DA_STRATEGY: Director[%zu]: pk=%s stake=%\" PRIu64,\n                  i, hex, epoch->directors[i].stake);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\nstatic int\nda_compare_director(const void *a, const void *b)\n{\n    const da_director_t *da = a;\n    const da_director_t *db = b;\n\n    /* Descending by stake, tiebreaker: pubkey lexicographically */\n    if (da->stake > db->stake) return -1;\n    if (da->stake < db->stake) return  1;\n    return memcmp(da->pubkey, db->pubkey, sizeof(da->pubkey));\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Strategy Interface V-Table\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic const consensus_strategy_vtbl_t da_vtbl = {\n    .init           = da_init,\n    .free           = da_free,\n    .validate_block = da_validate_block,\n    .apply_block    = da_apply_block,\n    .name           = \"Delegated-Artistry\"\n};\n\n/*\n * Public factory exported by da_strategy.h\n */\nconsensus_strategy_t *\nda_strategy_create(void)\n{\n    consensus_strategy_t *s = calloc(1, sizeof(*s));\n    if (!s) {\n        LOG_SYSERROR(errno, \"DA_STRATEGY: Allocation failure\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n    s->vtbl      = &da_vtbl;\n    s->impl_ctx  = NULL;\n    return s;\n}","numLines":521,"startLine":1,"totalLines":521}}}

The strategy is loaded at runtime through a\n *   Strategy-Pattern interface (see pos_strategy.h) and receives a stream of\n *   ledger events via the internal Event-Bus.  The PoS module is responsible\n *   for:\n *\n *     • Managing validator stake tables\n *     • Electing the next block proposer with a stake-weighted VRF\n *     • Verifying block signatures and stake lock-ups\n *     • Distributing block rewards and slashing misbehaving validators\n *\n *   The implementation is production-grade and is optimised for readability\n *   and robustness rather than raw performance.  Critical sections are\n *   protected by pthread mutexes; cryptographic operations rely on libsodium.\n *\n *   NOTE: The surrounding infrastructure (ledger storage, event bus, etc.)\n *         is mocked by forward declarations to keep this file self-contained.\n *         In the full code-base these come from their respective headers.\n *\n * License:\n *   Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#include <assert.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <sodium.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include \"pos_strategy.h\"  /* Public interface */\n#include \"../include/crypto_utils.h\"      /* crypto_sign_block(), hash(), … */\n#include \"../include/event_bus.h\"         /* event_bus_publish(), … */\n#include \"../include/ledger_storage.h\"    /* ledger_get_balance(), … */\n#include \"../include/logger.h\"            /* HC_LOG_DEBUG(), … */\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                              Local  Types                                 *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* Validator entry kept in memory; periodically snapshotted to disk */\ntypedef struct validator_entry_s\n{\n    wallet_addr_t  address;        /* 32-byte public key (bech32-decoded) */\n    uint64_t       stake;          /* Currently bonded stake              */\n    uint64_t       locked_until;   /* Epoch height until which stake is locked */\n    uint64_t       rewards;        /* Pending rewards (lazy withdrawn)    */\n    bool           jailed;         /* true when validator is slashed      */\n} validator_entry_t;\n\n/* Main PoS context – one per service instance */\ntypedef struct pos_ctx_s\n{\n    validator_entry_t *validators; /* Dynamic array                       */\n    size_t             v_len;      /* Elements in the array               */\n    size_t             v_cap;      /* Capacity                            */\n\n    uint64_t           total_stake;/* Aggregate bonded stake              */\n    uint64_t           current_epoch;\n    event_bus_t       *bus;        /* Weak ref to global Event-Bus        */\n\n    pthread_rwlock_t   rwlock;     /* Protects validators & total_stake   */\n} pos_ctx_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                       Forward Declarations / Helpers                      *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic int      ensure_capacity(pos_ctx_t *ctx);\nstatic ssize_t  find_validator(pos_ctx_t *ctx, const wallet_addr_t addr);\nstatic int      add_validator(pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n                              const wallet_addr_t addr,\n                              uint64_t stake);\n\nstatic int      vrf_elect(const pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n                          const uint8_t previous_block_hash[crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES],\n                          wallet_addr_t out_selected);\n\nstatic int      apply_block_rewards(pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n                                    const wallet_addr_t proposer,\n                                    uint64_t block_fees);\n\nstatic int      slash_validator(pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n                                const wallet_addr_t address,\n                                const char *reason);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                       Public  Interface  (Strategy)                       *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic pos_ctx_t g_ctx;  /* Singleton instance – strategy is process-local */\n\n\n/*\n * pos_strategy_init()\n * -------------------\n * Initialise libsodium (if necessary), internal state and subscribe to the\n * Event-Bus streams required by the PoS module.\n */\nint\npos_strategy_init(event_bus_t *bus)\n{\n    if (sodium_init() == -1) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"[PoS] libsodium init failed\\n\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    memset(&g_ctx, 0, sizeof(g_ctx));\n    g_ctx.bus = bus;\n    pthread_rwlock_init(&g_ctx.rwlock, NULL);\n\n    /* Pre-allocate validator table with a sane default */\n    g_ctx.v_cap = 64;\n    g_ctx.validators = calloc(g_ctx.v_cap, sizeof(validator_entry_t));\n    if (!g_ctx.validators) {\n        perror(\"[PoS] calloc validators\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Subscribe to ledger events (mocked) */\n    event_bus_subscribe(bus, EVENT_STAKE_BOND,  pos_strategy_on_event);\n    event_bus_subscribe(bus, EVENT_STAKE_UNBOND,pos_strategy_on_event);\n    event_bus_subscribe(bus, EVENT_NEW_BLOCK,   pos_strategy_on_event);\n\n    HC_LOG_INFO(\"[PoS] strategy initialised\");\n    return 0;\n}\n\n\n/*\n * pos_strategy_shutdown()\n * -----------------------\n * Flush any in-memory state to durable storage and cleanup allocations.\n */\nvoid\npos_strategy_shutdown(void)\n{\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    /* In a real implementation we would persist validator snapshots here */\n\n    free(g_ctx.validators);\n    g_ctx.validators = NULL;\n    g_ctx.v_len      = 0;\n    g_ctx.v_cap      = 0;\n    g_ctx.total_stake= 0;\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n    pthread_rwlock_destroy(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    HC_LOG_INFO(\"[PoS] strategy shut down\");\n}\n\n\n/*\n * pos_strategy_on_event()\n * -----------------------\n * Central entry point for Event-Bus callbacks.  Handles three primary event\n * classes: stake bond/unbond, and new block arrival.\n */\nvoid\npos_strategy_on_event(const event_t *ev)\n{\n    switch (ev->type) {\n\n    case EVENT_STAKE_BOND:\n        pos_strategy_handle_bond((const stake_event_t *)ev->payload);\n        break;\n\n    case EVENT_STAKE_UNBOND:\n        pos_strategy_handle_unbond((const stake_event_t *)ev->payload);\n        break;\n\n    case EVENT_NEW_BLOCK:\n        pos_strategy_handle_block((const block_event_t *)ev->payload);\n        break;\n\n    default:\n        HC_LOG_WARN(\"[PoS] unknown event type: %d\", (int)ev->type);\n    }\n}\n\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                          Event  Handlers                                  *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/*\n * Handle stake bonding.  Updates validator table and total stake.\n */\nvoid\npos_strategy_handle_bond(const stake_event_t *ev)\n{\n    int rc;\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    ssize_t idx = find_validator(&g_ctx, ev->staker);\n    if (idx < 0) {\n        /* New validator */\n        rc = add_validator(&g_ctx, ev->staker, ev->amount);\n        if (rc != 0) {\n            HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] failed to add validator\");\n            pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n            return;\n        }\n    } else {\n        validator_entry_t *v = &g_ctx.validators[idx];\n        v->stake += ev->amount;\n        v->locked_until = g_ctx.current_epoch + POS_MIN_LOCK_EPOCHS;\n    }\n\n    g_ctx.total_stake += ev->amount;\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    HC_LOG_DEBUG(\"[PoS] bonded %\" PRIu64 \" to validator %.8s…, total=%\" PRIu64,\n                 ev->amount, ev->staker, g_ctx.total_stake);\n}\n\n\n/*\n * Handle stake unbonding request.  Stake is not immediately released: we place\n * it in a cooldown queue (omitted for brevity) and unlock once the holding\n * period expires.\n */\nvoid\npos_strategy_handle_unbond(const stake_event_t *ev)\n{\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    ssize_t idx = find_validator(&g_ctx, ev->staker);\n    if (idx < 0) {\n        HC_LOG_WARN(\"[PoS] unbond from unknown validator %.8s…\", ev->staker);\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    validator_entry_t *v = &g_ctx.validators[idx];\n    if (v->stake < ev->amount) {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] unbond exceeds stake\");\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    v->stake -= ev->amount;\n    g_ctx.total_stake -= ev->amount;\n\n    /* TODO: Push to cooldown queue for delayed release */\n\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    HC_LOG_DEBUG(\"[PoS] unbonded %\" PRIu64 \" from validator %.8s…, remaining=%\"\n                 PRIu64, ev->amount, ev->staker, v->stake);\n}\n\n\n/*\n * Handle new block arrival.  Verify proposer signature and stake, distribute\n * fees and reward, then increment epoch if necessary.\n */\nvoid\npos_strategy_handle_block(const block_event_t *ev)\n{\n    const block_t *blk = &ev->block;\n\n    /* 1. Verify signature */\n    if (crypto_verify_block(blk) != 0) {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] invalid block signature\");\n        /* TODO: Reject block on the ledger pipeline */\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* 2. Ensure proposer is valid and not jailed */\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n    ssize_t idx = find_validator(&g_ctx, blk->proposer);\n    if (idx < 0) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] proposer %.8s… not in validator set\", blk->proposer);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const validator_entry_t *v = &g_ctx.validators[idx];\n    if (v->jailed) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] jailed proposer %.8s…\", blk->proposer);\n        /* Slash attempt */\n        slash_validator(&g_ctx, blk->proposer, \"Proposed block while jailed\");\n        return;\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    /* 3. Apply rewards */\n    if (apply_block_rewards(&g_ctx, blk->proposer, blk->fees) != 0) {\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] reward distribution failed\");\n    }\n\n    /* 4. Advance epoch if block boundary crossed */\n    if (blk->height % POS_BLOCKS_PER_EPOCH == 0) {\n        g_ctx.current_epoch++;\n        HC_LOG_INFO(\"[PoS] epoch advanced to %\" PRIu64, g_ctx.current_epoch);\n    }\n}\n\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                    Consensus / Validator Selection                        *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/*\n * pos_strategy_select_proposer()\n * ------------------------------\n * Called by the block-production pipeline to pick the next validator who\n * is eligible to create a block.  Uses a VRF to achieve randomness that\n * can be independently verified by all peers.\n *\n * Returns 0 on success and fills `out_addr`.\n */\nint\npos_strategy_select_proposer(const uint8_t prev_block_hash[crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES],\n                             wallet_addr_t out_addr)\n{\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n    if (g_ctx.total_stake == 0 || g_ctx.v_len == 0) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n        HC_LOG_ERROR(\"[PoS] no stake available for selection\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    int rc = vrf_elect(&g_ctx, prev_block_hash, out_addr);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_ctx.rwlock);\n\n    return rc;\n}\n\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n *                        Internal helper functions                          *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/*\n * ensure_capacity()\n * -----------------\n * Grow the validator array if necessary.\n */\nstatic int\nensure_capacity(pos_ctx_t *ctx)\n{\n    if (ctx->v_len < ctx->v_cap)\n        return 0;\n\n    size_t new_cap = ctx->v_cap * 2;\n    validator_entry_t *tmp = realloc(ctx->validators,\n                                     new_cap * sizeof(validator_entry_t));\n    if (!tmp) {\n        perror(\"[PoS] realloc validators\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n    ctx->validators = tmp;\n    ctx->v_cap = new_cap;\n    return 0;\n}\n\n\n/*\n * find_validator()\n * ----------------\n * Return the index of a validator in the array, or ‑1 if not present.\n * Linear search is acceptable for few hundred validators; for larger sets an\n * ordered map should be used (omitted for brevity).\n */\nstatic ssize_t\nfind_validator(pos_ctx_t *ctx, const wallet_addr_t addr)\n{\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ctx->v_len; ++i) {\n        if (memcmp(ctx->validators[i].address, addr, WALLET_ADDR_LEN) == 0)\n            return (ssize_t)i;\n    }\n    return -1;\n}\n\n\n/*\n * add_validator()\n * ---------------\n * Append a new validator to the table.\n */\nstatic int\nadd_validator(pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n              const wallet_addr_t addr,\n              uint64_t stake)\n{\n    if (ensure_capacity(ctx) != 0)\n        return -1;\n\n    validator_entry_t *v = &ctx->validators[ctx->v_len++];\n    memset(v, 0, sizeof(*v));\n    memcpy(v->address, addr, WALLET_ADDR_LEN);\n    v->stake        = stake;\n    v->locked_until = ctx->current_epoch + POS_MIN_LOCK_EPOCHS;\n    v->jailed       = false;\n\n    HC_LOG_INFO(\"[PoS] new validator %.8s…, stake=%\" PRIu64,\n                addr, stake);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n\n/*\n * vrf_elect()\n * -----------\n * Perform stake-weighted random election using libsodium's crypto_core_ed25519.\n * The VRF simulates a beacon: H = Hash(prev_hash || validator_pub || epoch).\n * Each validator computes H and compares it with a threshold derived from\n * stake/total.  The smallest valid H wins.  For centralised selection (this\n * service) we compute H for all candidates.\n */\nstatic int\nvrf_elect(const pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n          const uint8_t prev_block_hash[crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES],\n          wallet_addr_t out_selected)\n{\n    uint8_t min_hash[crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES];\n    bool    selected = false;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ctx->v_len; ++i) {\n        const validator_entry_t *v = &ctx->validators[i];\n        if (v->jailed || v->stake == 0)\n            continue;\n\n        /* Beacon: hash(prev_hash | validator_pub | epoch_le) */\n        uint8_t buf[crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES + WALLET_ADDR_LEN + sizeof(uint64_t)];\n        size_t  off = 0;\n        memcpy(buf + off, prev_block_hash, crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES); off += crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES;\n        memcpy(buf + off, v->address, WALLET_ADDR_LEN);               off += WALLET_ADDR_LEN;\n        uint64_t epoch_le = htole64(ctx->current_epoch);\n        memcpy(buf + off, &epoch_le, sizeof(epoch_le));\n\n        uint8_t h[crypto_hash_sha256_BYTES];\n        crypto_hash_sha256(h, buf, sizeof(buf));\n\n        /* Convert leading 16 bytes to a 128-bit integer for comparison */\n        if (!selected || memcmp(h, min_hash, 16) < 0) {\n            memcpy(min_hash, h, sizeof(min_hash));\n            memcpy(out_selected, v->address, WALLET_ADDR_LEN);\n            selected = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!selected)\n        return -1;\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n\n/*\n * apply_block_rewards()\n * ---------------------\n * Credit proposer with block reward plus fees, and distribute a portion to\n * delegators (omitted for brevity).\n */\nstatic int\napply_block_rewards(pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n                    const wallet_addr_t proposer,\n                    uint64_t block_fees)\n{\n    static const uint64_t BASE_REWARD = 5 * HC_COINS; /* 5 tokens per block */\n\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n    ssize_t idx = find_validator(ctx, proposer);\n    if (idx < 0) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    validator_entry_t *v = &ctx->validators[idx];\n    v->rewards += BASE_REWARD + block_fees;\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n\n    /* Emit reward event */\n    reward_event_t rev = {\n        .validator = {0},\n        .amount    = BASE_REWARD + block_fees\n    };\n    memcpy(rev.validator, proposer, WALLET_ADDR_LEN);\n    event_t ev = {\n        .type    = EVENT_REWARD,\n        .payload = &rev\n    };\n    event_bus_publish(ctx->bus, &ev);\n\n    HC_LOG_DEBUG(\"[PoS] rewarded %.8s… with %\" PRIu64,\n                 proposer, rev.amount);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n\n/*\n * slash_validator()\n * -----------------\n * Slash a validator for misbehaviour by burning a percentage of its stake and\n * jailing it for several epochs.\n */\nstatic int\nslash_validator(pos_ctx_t *ctx,\n                const wallet_addr_t address,\n                const char *reason)\n{\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n    ssize_t idx = find_validator(ctx, address);\n    if (idx < 0) {\n        pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    validator_entry_t *v = &ctx->validators[idx];\n    uint64_t penalty = v->stake / POS_SLASH_FRACTION;\n    v->stake -= penalty;\n    v->jailed = true;\n\n    ctx->total_stake -= penalty;\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&ctx->rwlock);\n\n    HC_LOG_WARN(\"[PoS] validator %.8s… slashed by %\" PRIu64 \" (%s)\",\n                address, penalty, reason);\n\n    /* Emit slashing event */\n    slash_event_t sev = {\n        .validator = {0},\n        .amount    = penalty,\n        .reason    = {0}\n    };\n    memcpy(sev.validator, address, WALLET_ADDR_LEN);\n    strncpy(sev.reason, reason, sizeof(sev.reason)-1);\n\n    event_t ev = {\n        .type    = EVENT_SLASH,\n        .payload = &sev\n    };\n    event_bus_publish(ctx->bus, &ev);\n\n    return ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* build_block()\n * Take a snapshot of tx-pool and compose a ledger_block ready for consensus.\n */\nstatic ledger_err_t\nbuild_block(struct block_builder *ctx, struct ledger_block *out_block)\n{\n    if (!ctx || !out_block) return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n\n    size_t snapshot_size = ctx->cfg.max_txs_per_block\n                               ? ctx->cfg.max_txs_per_block\n                               : TX_POOL_MAX_POP_BULK;\n\n    tx_list_t *txs = tx_pool_pop_bulk(ctx->pool, snapshot_size);\n    if (!txs) {\n        return LEDGER_EEMPTY; /* No pending transactions */\n    }\n\n    memset(out_block, 0, sizeof *out_block);\n\n    /* Fill block header */\n    out_block->header.version        = LEDGER_BLOCK_VERSION;\n    out_block->header.timestamp_ms   = unix_epoch_ms();\n    out_block->header.height         = consensus_next_height(ctx->consensus);\n    out_block->header.prev_blockhash = consensus_last_hash(ctx->consensus);\n\n    /* Merkle root */\n    ledger_err_t err = compute_merkle_root(txs, out_block->header.merkle_root);\n    if (err != LEDGER_OK) {\n        tx_list_free(txs);\n        return err;\n    }\n\n    /* Copy txs to block body */\n    out_block->txs      = txs;\n    out_block->tx_count = txs->count;\n\n    /* Finalise block hash */\n    crypto_hash256_ctx hctx;\n    crypto_hash256_init(&hctx);\n    crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, &out_block->header, sizeof out_block->header);\n    crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, out_block->header.merkle_root,\n                          sizeof out_block->header.merkle_root);\n    crypto_hash256_final(&hctx, out_block->block_hash);\n\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Merkle tree                                                               *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic ledger_err_t\ncompute_merkle_root(const tx_list_t *txs, hash256_t out_root)\n{\n    if (!txs || txs->count == 0) {\n        return LEDGER_EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    size_t leaf_count = txs->count;\n    size_t level_count = leaf_count;\n\n    /* Allocate working buffer – power of two rounding */\n    size_t capacity = 1;\n    while (capacity < leaf_count) capacity <<= 1;\n    hash256_t *buf = calloc(capacity, sizeof(hash256_t));\n    if (!buf) return LEDGER_ENOMEM;\n\n    /* Copy leaves (tx hashes) */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < leaf_count; ++i) {\n        memcpy(buf[i], txs->items[i]->tx_hash, sizeof(hash256_t));\n    }\n    /* Pad duplicate of last leaf if odd count */\n    for (size_t i = leaf_count; i < capacity; ++i) {\n        memcpy(buf[i], buf[leaf_count - 1], sizeof(hash256_t));\n    }\n\n    /* Iteratively hash pairs */\n    hash256_t tmp;\n    while (level_count > 1) {\n        size_t next_level = (level_count + 1) / 2;\n        for (size_t i = 0, j = 0; i < level_count; i += 2, ++j) {\n            crypto_hash256_ctx hctx;\n            crypto_hash256_init(&hctx);\n            crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, buf[i],   sizeof(hash256_t));\n            crypto_hash256_update(&hctx, buf[i+1], sizeof(hash256_t));\n            crypto_hash256_final(&hctx, tmp);\n            memcpy(buf[j], tmp, sizeof(hash256_t));\n        }\n        level_count = next_level;\n    }\n\n    memcpy(out_root, buf[0], sizeof(hash256_t));\n    free(buf);\n    return LEDGER_OK;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- *\n * Utilities                                                                 *\n * ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic inline uint64_t\nunix_epoch_ms(void)\n{\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    return (uint64_t)ts.tv_sec * 1000ULL + (uint64_t)(ts.tv_nsec / 1e6);\n}","numLines":334,"startLine":1,"totalLines":334}}}

202→    if (src >= end) return NULL;\n   203→    uint8_t prefix = *src++;\n   204→    if (prefix < 0xFDU) {\n   205→        *v_out = prefix;\n   206→        return src;\n   207→    } else if (prefix 226→/* Compute double-SHA-256 */\n   227→static void _sha256d(const uint8_t *data, size_t len,\n   228→                     uint8_t hash_out[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE])\n   229→{\n   230→    uint8_t 236→static int _merkle_root(struct ledger_tx **txs, size_t n,\n   237→                        uint8_t root_out[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE])\n   238→{\n   239→    if (n == 0 || txs == NULL) return hashes[i * 2];\n   259→            const uint8_t *right = (i * 2 + 1 < level_count)\n   260→                                   ? hashes[i * 2 + 1]\n   261→                                   : hashes[i * 2];\n   262→            uint8_t concat[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE * 2];\n   263→            memcpy(concat, left,  BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n   264→            memcpy(concat + BLOCK_HASH_SIZE, right, BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n   265→            _sha256d(concat, sizeof(concat), hashes[i]);\n   266→        }\n   267→        level_count = next_count;\n   268→    }\n   269→    memcpy(root_out, hashes[0], BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n   270→    free(hashes);\n   271→    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n   272→}\n   273→\n   274→/* Block header hashing */\n   275→static void _compute_block_hash(const block_header_t *hdr,\n   276→                                uint8_t hash_out[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE])\n   277→{\n   278→    _sha256d((const uint8_t *)hdr, sizeof(*hdr), hash_out);\n   279→}\n   280→\n   281→/* ----------  Public API Implementations  ---------- */\n   282→int block_init(block_t            *blk,\n   283→               const uint8_t       prev_hash[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE],\n   284→               uint64_t            height,\n   285→               uint32_t            bits)\n   286→{\n   287→    if (!blk) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n   288→    memset(blk, 0, sizeof(*blk));\n   289→\n   290→    blk->header.version = blk->tx_capacity  = _BLOCK_MIN_TX_CAPACITY;\n   301→    blk->txs          = calloc(blk->tx_capacity, sizeof(struct ledger_tx *));\n   302→    if (!blk->txs) return BLOCK_ERR_MEM;\n   303→\n   304→    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n   305→}\n   306→\n   307→int block_add_tx(block_t *blk, struct ledger_tx *tx)\n   308→{\n   309→    if (!blk || !tx) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n   310→    if (blk->finalized) 327→}\n   328→\n   329→int block_finalize(block_t *blk)\n   330→{\n   331→    if (!blk) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n   332→    if (blk->finalized) return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n   333→    if (blk->tx_count == 2\n\n/* Varint helpers (Bitcoin-style, compactSize) */\nstatic size_t _varint_encoded_size(uint64_t v)\n{\n    if (v < 0xFDULL)             return 1;\n    else if (v <= 0xFFFFULL)     return 3;\n    else if (v <= 0xFFFFFFFFULL) return 5;\n    else                         return 9;\n}\n\nstatic uint8_t *_varint_write(uint8_t *dst, uint64_t v)\n{\n    if (v < 0xFDULL) {\n        *dst++ = (uint8_t)v;\n    } else if (v <= 0xFFFFULL) {\n        *dst++ = 0xFD;\n        uint16_t le = (uint16_t)v;\n        memcpy(dst, &le, sizeof(le));\n        dst += sizeof(le);\n    } else if (v <= 0xFFFFFFFFULL) {\n        *dst++ = 0xFE;\n        uint32_t le = (uint32_t)v;\n        memcpy(dst, &le, sizeof(le));\n        dst += sizeof(le);\n    } else {\n        *dst++ = 0xFF;\n        uint64_t const uint8_t *end,\n                                   uint64_t *v_out)\n{\n    if (src >= end) return NULL;\n    uint8_t prefix = *src++;\n    if (prefix < 0xFDU) {\n        *v_out = prefix;\n        return src;\n    } else if (prefix == 0xFD) {\n        if (src + 2 > end) return NULL;\n        uint16_t le;\n        memcpy(&le, src, 2); src += 2;\n        *v_out = le;\n    } else if (prefix ==           uint8_t hash_out[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE])\n{\n    uint8_t tmp[SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH];\n    SHA256(data, len, tmp);\n    SHA256(tmp, sizeof(tmp), hash_out);\n}\n\n/* Merkle root calculation (simple, iterative) */\nstatic int _merkle_root(struct ledger_tx **txs, size_t n,\n                        uint8_t root_out[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE])\n{\n    if (n == 0 || txs == NULL) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n\n    /* Allocate scratch space for level hashing */\n    uint8_t (*hashes)[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE] = malloc(n * BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n    if (!hashes) return BLOCK_ERR_MEM;\n\n    /* Seed with transaction hashes */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (ledger_tx_hash(txs[i], hashes[i]) != 0) {\n            free(hashes);\n            return BLOCK_ERR_HASH;\n        }\n    }\n\n    size_t */\n            const uint8_t *left  = hashes[i * 2];\n            const uint8_t *right = (i * 2 + 1 < level_count)\n                                   ? hashes[i * 2 + 1]\n                                   : hashes[i * 2];\n            uint8_t concat[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE * 2];\n            memcpy(concat, left,  BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n            memcpy(concat + BLOCK_HASH_SIZE, right, BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n            _sha256d(concat, sizeof(concat), hashes[i]);\n        }\n        level_count = next_count;\n    }\n    memcpy(root_out, hashes[0], BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n    free(hashes);\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\n/* Block header hashing */\nstatic void _compute_block_hash(const block_header_t *hdr,\n                                uint8_t hash_out[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE])\n{\n    _sha256d((const uint8_t *)hdr, sizeof(*hdr), hash_out);\n}\n\n/* ----------  Public API Implementations  ---------- */\nint block_init(block_t            *blk,\n               const uint8_t       prev_hash[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE],\n               uint64_t            height,\n               uint32_t            bits)\n{\n    if (!blk) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n    memset(blk, 0, sizeof(*blk));\n\n    blk->header.version = BLOCK_DEFAULT_VERSION;\n\n    if (prev_hash)\n        memcpy(blk->header.prev_hash, prev_hash, BLOCK_HASH_SIZE);\n\n    blk->header.timestamp = (uint64_t)time(NULL);\n    blk->header.bits      = bits;\n    blk->header.nonce     = 0;          /* mined later */\n\n    blk->height       = height;\n    blk->tx_capacity  = _BLOCK_MIN_TX_CAPACITY;\n    blk->txs          = calloc(blk->tx_capacity, sizeof(struct ledger_tx *));\n    if (!blk->txs) return BLOCK_ERR_MEM;\n\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\nint block_add_tx(block_t *blk, struct ledger_tx *tx)\n{\n    if (!blk || !tx) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n    if (blk->finalized) return BLOCK_ERR_VALIDATION;\n    if (blk->tx_count >= BLOCK_MAX_TX) return BLOCK_ERR_TX_LIMIT;\n\n    /* Grow capacity if needed */\n    if (blk->tx_count == blk->tx_capacity) {\n        size_t new_cap = blk->tx_capacity * _BLOCK_GROWTH_FACTOR;\n        if (new_cap == 0) new_cap = _BLOCK_MIN_TX_CAPACITY;\n        struct ledger_tx **new_arr = realloc(blk->txs,\n                                    new_cap * sizeof(struct ledger_tx *));\n        if (!new_arr) return BLOCK_ERR_MEM;\n        blk->txs = new_arr;\n        blk->tx_capacity = new_cap;\n    }\n\n    blk->txs[blk->tx_count++] = tx;\n    blk->hash_valid = false;\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\nint block_finalize(block_t *blk)\n{\n    if (!blk) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n    if (blk->finalized) return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n    if (blk->tx_count == 0) return BLOCK_ERR_VALIDATION;\n\n    int rc = _merkle_root(blk->txs, blk->tx_count, blk->header.merkle_root);\n    if (rc != BLOCK_SUCCESS) return rc;\n\n    _compute_block_hash(&blk->header, blk->hash);\n    blk->hash_valid = true;\n    blk->finalized  = true;\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\nint block_validate(const block_t *blk)\n{\n    if (!blk || !blk->finalized) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n\n    /* Validate merkle root */\n    uint8_t root[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE];\n    int rc = _merkle_root((struct ledger_tx **)blk->txs,\n                          blk->tx_count, root);\n    if (rc != BLOCK_SUCCESS) return rc;\n    if (memcmp(root, blk->header.merkle_root, BLOCK_HASH_SIZE) != 0)\n        return BLOCK_ERR_VALIDATION;\n\n    /* Validate cached hash */\n    uint8_t calc_hash[BLOCK_HASH_SIZE];\n    _compute_block_hash(&blk->header, calc_hash);\n    if (memcmp(calc_hash, blk->hash, BLOCK_HASH_SIZE) != 0)\n        return BLOCK_ERR_HASH;\n\n    /* Validate each transaction (delegated) */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < blk->tx_count; ++i) {\n        /* Transaction-level validation stub.\n         * ledger_tx_validate() could be invoked here if available. */\n        (void)i;\n    }\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\nint block_serialize(const block_t *blk,\n                    uint8_t **buf_out, size_t *len_out)\n{\n    if (!blk || !buf_out || !len_out) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n    if (!blk->finalized) return BLOCK_ERR_VALIDATION;\n\n    /* First determine required buffer size */\n    size_t size = sizeof(block_header_t);\n    size += _varint_encoded_size(blk->tx_count);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < blk->tx_count; ++i) {\n        uint8_t *tx_buf = NULL; size_t tx_len = 0;\n        if (ledger_tx_serialize(blk->txs[i], &tx_buf, &tx_len) != 0)\n            return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n        size += _varint_encoded_size(tx_len) + tx_len;\n        free(tx_buf); /* length only */\n    }\n\n    uint8_t *buf = malloc(size);\n    if (!buf) return BLOCK_ERR_MEM;\n    uint8_t *ptr = buf;\n\n    /* Header */\n    memcpy(ptr, &blk->header, sizeof(blk->header));\n    ptr += sizeof(blk->header);\n\n    /* Transaction list length */\n    ptr = _varint_write(ptr, blk->tx_count);\n\n    /* Transactions */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < blk->tx_count; ++i) {\n        uint8_t *tx_buf = NULL; size_t tx_len = 0;\n        if (ledger_tx_serialize(blk->txs[i], &tx_buf, &tx_len) != 0) {\n            free(buf);\n            return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n        }\n        ptr = _varint_write(ptr, tx_len);\n        memcpy(ptr, tx_buf, tx_len);\n        ptr += tx_len;\n        free(tx_buf);\n    }\n\n    /* Sanity check */\n    if ((size_t)(ptr - buf) != size) {\n        free(buf);\n        return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n    }\n\n    *buf_out = buf;\n    *len_out = size;\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\nint block_deserialize(const uint8_t *buf, size_t buf_len,\n                      block_t *blk_out)\n{\n    if (!buf || !blk_out) return BLOCK_ERR_PARAM;\n    const uint8_t *ptr = buf, *end = buf + buf_len;\n\n    if (ptr + sizeof(block_header_t) > end) return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n    block_header_t hdr;\n    memcpy(&hdr, ptr, sizeof(hdr));\n    ptr += sizeof(hdr);\n\n    /* Read tx_count */\n    uint64_t tx_count = 0;\n    ptr = _varint_read(ptr, end, &tx_count);\n    if (!ptr) return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n    if (tx_count > BLOCK_MAX_TX) return BLOCK_ERR_TX_LIMIT;\n\n    /* Init block */\n    int rc = block_init(blk_out, hdr.prev_hash, 0 /* height unknown */, hdr.bits);\n    if (rc != BLOCK_SUCCESS) return rc;\n    blk_out->header = hdr;  /* Override timestamp, etc. */\n    blk_out->tx_count = 0;  /* Will be rebuilt */\n\n    /* Deserialize each transaction */\n    for (uint64_t i = 0; i < tx_count; ++i) {\n        uint64_t tx_len = 0;\n        ptr = _varint_read(ptr, end, &tx_len);\n        if (!ptr || ptr + tx_len > end) {\n            block_free(blk_out);\n            return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n        }\n        struct ledger_tx *tx = NULL;\n        if (ledger_tx_deserialize(ptr, (size_t)tx_len, &tx) != 0) {\n            block_free(blk_out);\n            return BLOCK_ERR_SERIALIZATION;\n        }\n        ptr += tx_len;\n        rc = block_add_tx(blk_out, tx);\n        if (rc != BLOCK_SUCCESS) {\n            ledger_tx_free(tx);\n            block_free(blk_out);\n            return rc;\n        }\n    }\n\n    blk_out->finalized  = true;\n    _compute_block_hash(&blk_out->header, blk_out->hash);\n    blk_out->hash_valid = true;\n    return BLOCK_SUCCESS;\n}\n\nvoid block_free(block_t *blk)\n{\n    if (!blk) return;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < blk->tx_count; ++i) {\n        ledger_tx_free(blk->txs[i]);\n    }\n    free(blk->txs);\n    memset(blk, 0, sizeof(*blk));\n}\n\n#endif /* BLOCK_IMPLEMENTATION */\n#endif /* The format is forward-compatible through an explicit\n *         version field embedded in the transaction header.\n *      •  All heap allocations are zeroed before free() to avoid leaking\n *         sensitive key material.\n *\n *  Copyright:\n *      © 2023-2024 HoloCanvas Contributors.  MIT License.\n */\n\n#ifndef HOLOCANVAS_LEDGER_CORE_TRANSACTION_H\n#define HOLOCANVAS_LEDGER_CORE_TRANSACTION_H\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *          32u\n#define HC_TX_MEMO_BYTES_MAX       1024u\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Error Codes\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\ntypedef enum {\n    HC_TX_OK                       = 0,\n    HC_TX_ERR_NOMEM               = -1,\n */\n    uint8_t  script_hash[HC_SCRIPT_HASH_BYTES];       /* optional script */\n} hc_tx_output_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    /* Header */\n    uint32_t   version;                               /* le32 */\n    hc_tx_type_t type;                                /* uint8 */\n    uint64_t   timestamp;                             /* unix epoch, le64 */\n    uint8_t    prev_block_hash[HC_TX_HASH_BYTES]; */\nstatic inline void\nhc_tx_zero(hc_transaction_t *tx)\n{\n    if (tx) {\n        sodium_memzero(tx, sizeof(*tx));\n    }\n}\n\n/*\n *  Creates an empty transaction on the heap.\n *  The resulting ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstatic inline void\n_write_le32(uint8_t **cursor, uint32_t v)\n{\n    uint32_t le = htole32(v);\n    memcpy(*cursor, &le, sizeof(le));\n    *cursor += sizeof(le);\n}\nstatic inline void\n_write_le64(uint8_t **cursor, uint64_t v)\n{\n    uint64_t le = htole64(v);\n    memcpy(*cursor, &le, sizeof(le));\n    *cursor += sizeof(le);\n}\nstatic inline void\n_write_mem(uint8_t **cursor, const void *src, size_t len)\n{\n    memcpy(*cursor, src, len);\n    *cursor += len;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  API – Serialization\n *      Serializes a transaction into a newly allocated buffer.\n *      Caller must free(*out_buf) after use (via sodium_free()).\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstatic inline hc_tx_err_t\nhc_tx_serialize(const hc_transaction_t *tx, uint8_t **out_buf, size_t *out_len)\n{\n    if (!tx || !out_buf || !out_len) return HC_TX_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;\n\n    /* Basic size calculation */\n    size_t sz = 0;\n    sz += sizeof(tx->version)\n        + sizeof(tx->type)\n        + sizeof(tx->timestamp)\n        + HC_TX_HASH_BYTES         /* prev_block_hash */\n        + sizeof(tx->input_count)\n        + sizeof(tx->output_count);\n\n    sz += *cursor++ = tx->input_count;\n    *cursor++ = tx->output_count;\n\n    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < tx->input_count; ++i) {\n        const hc_tx_input_t *in = &tx->inputs[i];\n        _write_mem(&cursor, ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  API – Hashing\n *      Calculates the Blake2b hash of the serialized transaction and caches\n *      it in (crypto_generichash(tx->tx_hash,\n                           sizeof(tx->tx_hash),\n                           buf,\n                           len,\n                           NULL, 0) != 0) {\n        sodium_free(buf);\n        return HC_TX_ERR_CRYPTO;\n    }\n\n    tx->hash_valid = true;\n    sodium_free(buf);\n    return HC_TX_OK;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  API – Verification\n *      Verifies all attached signatures and cached hash.\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstatic inline hc_tx_err_t\nhc_tx_verify(const hc_transaction_t *tx)\n{\n    if (!tx) return HC_TX_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;\n\n    /* Hash check */\n    uint8_t recomputed[HC_TX_HASH_BYTES];\n    hc_transaction_t tmp = *tx;\n    tmp.hash_valid = false;\n    if (hc_tx_calculate_hash(&tmp) != HC_TX_OK)\n        return                  mgr->engine.name);\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    /* Spawn worker thread */\n    if (pthread_create(&mgr->worker, NULL,\n                       _worker_loop, mgr) != 0) {\n        logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_ERR,\n                   \"[ConsensusMgr] Failed to spawn worker thread: %s\",\n                   strerror(errno));\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_INFO,\n               \"[ConsensusMgr] Started with engine: %s\",\n               mgr->engine.name);\n    return mgr;\n\nfail:\n    _unload_engine(mgr);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&mgr->q_mutex);\n    pthread_cond_destroy(&mgr->q_cond);\n    free(mgr);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\nvoid consensus_manager_destroy(consensus_manager_t *mgr)\n{\n    if (!mgr) return;\n\n    /* Request worker shutdown */\n pthread_join(mgr->worker, NULL);\n\n    /* Unload engine */\n    if (mgr->engine.shutdown) {\n        mgr->engine.shutdown();\n    }\n    _unload_engine(mgr);\n\n    /* Drain any remaining queued ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */\n\nstatic void _enqueue_block(consensus_manager_t *mgr, block_t *blk)\n{\n    struct blk_node *node = calloc(1, sizeof(*node));\n    if (!node) {\n        logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_ERR,\n                   \"[ConsensusMgr] OOM while enqueueing block\");\n        return;\n    }\n    node->blk = blk;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n    if (mgr->q_tail) {\n        mgr->q_tail->next = node;\n        mgr->q_tail       = node;\n    } else {\n        mgr->q_head = mgr->q_tail = node;\n    }\n    pthread_cond_signal(&mgr->q_cond);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n}\n\nstatic block_t *_dequeue_block(consensus_manager_t *mgr)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&mgr->q_mutex);\n    while node->blk;\n    free(node);\n    return blk;\n}\n\nstatic void *_worker_loop(void *arg)\n{\n    consensus_manager_t *mgr = arg;\n\n    for (;;)\n    {\n        block_t *blk = _dequeue_block(mgr);\n        if (!blk) break; /* Shutdown requested */\n\n        /* Step 1: Verify candidate block */\n        char *err_msg = NULL;\n        if (!mgr->engine.verify_block(blk, &err_msg)) {\n details)\");\n            free(err_msg);\n            free(blk);\n            continue; /* Reject block */\n        }\n\n        /* Step 2: Finalise (exec state transitions) */\n        uint8_t new_state_root[32] = {0};\n        if (!mgr->engine.finalise_block(blk,\n                                        new_state_root,\n                                        &err_msg)) {\n            logger_log(LOG_LEVEL_ERR,\n                       \"[ConsensusMgr] Block@%lu finalisation failed: %s\",\n                       blk->height,\n                       err_msg ? err_msg : \"(no details)\");\n            free(err_msg);\n            free(blk);\n            continue; /* Inconsistent state, do not commit */\n        }\n\n        /* Step 3: Commit to durable storage */\n        if (!ledger_storage_put_block(mgr->storage, blk) ||\n            !ledger_storage_set_state_root(mgr->storage,\n                                           new_state_root)) {\n new_state_root[0], new_state_root[1]);\n\n        free(blk);\n    }\n\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Dynamic loading of Consensus Engine shared-library        */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic bool _load_engine(consensus_manager_t *mgr,\n                         const char It is responsible for:\n *     1. Verifying basic and contextual validity of incoming transactions.\n *     2. Applying state-transitions to the on-chain ledger representation.\n *     3. Emitting domain events for downstream micro-services (Mint-Factory,\n *        Governance-Hall, etc.) via the event-bus.\n *     4. Surfacing metrics for observability (Prometheus/OpenTelemetry).\n *\n *   The code purposefully avoids any blockchain-consensus specifics; it only\n *   performs deterministic, replayable ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#ifndef HLC_TX_PROCESSOR_MAX_SCRIPT_SIZE\n#define HLC_TX_PROCESSOR_MAX_SCRIPT_SIZE 8192U /* bytes */\n#endif\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Internal structures                                                      */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/* Processing statistics (hot in write-path, kept in memory) */\ntypedef struct txp_stats_s\n{\n    uint64_t accepted;\n    uint64_t rejected_sig;\n    uint64_t rejected_nonce;\n    uint64_t rejected_balance;\n    uint64_t rejected_payload;\n} txp_stats_t;\n\n/*\n * Transaction Processor context.\n * One instance per LedgerCore node – shared across worker threads.\n */\ntypedef struct txp_ctx_s\n{\n    ledger_t       *ledger;\n    event_bus_t    *bus;\n    txp_stats_t     stats;\n    metrics_handle_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx, uint64_t ts_unix);\nstatic void   tp_emit_event(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx);\nstatic void   tp_record_metrics(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const char *label, uint64_t dur_ns);\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Public API                                                               */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * tp_init()\n *   Allocate and initialise a Transaction Processor context.\n */\ntxp_ctx_t *tp_init(ledger_t *ledger, event_bus_t *bus)\n{\n    if (!ledger || !bus) {\n        log_error(\"tp_init: invalid argument (ledger/bus)\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    txp_ctx_t *ctx = calloc(1, sizeof(*ctx));\n    if (!ctx) {\n        log_error(\"tp_init: calloc failed (%s)\", strerror(errno));\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    ctx->ledger = ledger;\n    ctx->bus    = bus;\n    pthread_mutex_init(&ctx->mtx, NULL);\n\n    /* Register metrics */\n    ctx->m_metrics = metrics_register_namespace(\"ledger_core_tx_processor\");\n    if (ctx->m_metrics == METRICS_HANDLE_INVALID) {\n        log_error(\"tp_init: failed to register metrics namespace\");\n        /* Continue; metrics are optional */\n    }\n\n    log_info(\"Transaction Processor initialised.\");\n    return ctx;\n}\n\n/*\n * tp_shutdown()\n *   Free processor resources.\n */\nvoid tp_shutdown(txp_ctx_t **pctx)\n{\n    if (!pctx || !*pctx) return;\n\n    txp_ctx_t *ctx = *pctx;\n\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&ctx->mtx);\n    free(ctx);\n    *pctx = NULL;\n\n    log_info(\"Transaction Processor shutdown complete.\");\n}\n\n/*\n * tp_process()\n *   Entry-point for one transaction.  Thread-safe.\n *\n *   Returns:\n *     0  – transaction accepted & applied\n *    >0 – transaction rejected (status code defined below)\n *    <0 – fatal/internal error\n */\nint tp_process(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const uint8_t *raw, size_t raw_len)\n{\n    if (!ctx || !raw || !raw_len) {\n        return -EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    /* Decode/deserialize */\n    tx_t tx;\n    memset(&tx, 0, sizeof(tx));\n    if (tx_decode(&tx, raw, raw_len) != 0) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_process: failed to decode transaction (len=%zu)\", raw_len);\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_payload++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 4; /* invalid payload */\n    }\n\n    const uint64_t ts_unix = (uint64_t)time(NULL);\n    const uint64_t t0_ns   = metrics_now_ns();\n\n    /* Validation pipeline */\n    if (!tp_verify_signature(&tx)) {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_sig++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 1; /* bad signature */\n    }\n    if (!tp_verify_nonce(ctx, &tx)) {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_nonce++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 2; /* nonce mismatch */\n    }\n    if (!tp_verify_funds(ctx, &tx)) {\n        pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n        ctx->stats.rejected_balance++;\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n        return 3; /* insufficient funds */\n    }\n\n    /* ── Critical section: apply & emit event ───────────────────────────── */\n    if (tp_apply(ctx, &tx, ts_unix) != 0) {\n        return -EIO; /* internal error applying */\n    }\n\n    tp_emit_event(ctx, &tx);\n    tp_record_metrics(ctx, \"accepted\", metrics_now_ns() - t0_ns);\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n    ctx->stats.accepted++;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n\n    return 0; /* success */\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Validation helpers                                                       */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * Signature verification\n */\nstatic bool tp_verify_signature(const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    if (!tx) return false;\n\n    /* Ed25519 public key & signature size are fixed (32 / 64 bytes) */\n    if (tx->sig_len != 64 || tx->pubkey_len != 32) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_signature: malformed key/sig\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Hash the canonicalised, signature-free frame of the transaction */\n    uint8_t digest[32];\n    if (sha3_256(tx->frame, tx->frame_len, digest) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_verify_signature: sha3_256 failed\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    const bool ok = ed25519_verify(tx->signature, digest, sizeof(digest),\n                                   tx->pubkey);\n    if (!ok) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_signature: invalid signature for tx %s\", tx->tx_id);\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/*\n * Nonce verification\n *   Protects against replay and preserves causal order per-account.\n */\nstatic bool tp_verify_nonce(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    account_state_t acct;\n    if (ledger_get_account_state(ctx->ledger, tx->from, &acct) != 0) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_nonce: unknown account %s\", tx->from);\n        return false; /* account must exist */\n    }\n\n    const bool ok = (tx->nonce == acct.next_nonce);\n    if (!ok) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_nonce: expected nonce=%\" PRIu64 \", got=%\" PRIu64,\n                  acct.next_nonce, tx->nonce);\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/*\n * Balance / funds verification\n */\nstatic bool tp_verify_funds(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    account_state_t acct;\n    if (ledger_get_account_state(ctx->ledger, tx->from, &acct) != 0) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Gas cost: base + payload-dependent */\n    const uint64_t gas_cost = tx->gas_price * tx->gas_limit;\n    const uint64_t total    = gas_cost + tx->value;\n\n    const bool ok = (acct.balance >= total);\n    if (!ok) {\n        log_debug(\"tp_verify_funds: balance %\" PRIu64\n                  \" < required %\" PRIu64 \" (gas=%\" PRIu64 \", value=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n                  acct.balance, total, gas_cost, tx->value);\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Application / state-mutation                                             */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * tp_apply()\n *   Mutate ledger state and any domain-level state-machines.\n *\n *   NOTE: called inside the global mutex for now.  In production deployments\n *   we may employ sharded, fine-grained locks or MVCC to improve throughput.\n */\nstatic int tp_apply(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx, uint64_t ts_unix)\n{\n    int rc = 0;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&ctx->mtx);\n\n    /* Append ledger entry – durable write-ahead log (WAL) */\n    ledger_entry_t entry = {\n        .timestamp  = ts_unix,\n        .tx_id      = {0},\n        .raw        = tx->raw,\n        .raw_len    = tx->raw_len\n    };\n    memcpy(entry.tx_id, tx->tx_id, sizeof(entry.tx_id));\n\n    if ((rc = ledger_append(ctx->ledger, &entry)) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_append failed (rc=%d)\", rc);\n        goto exit;\n    }\n\n    /* Update account nonce & balance */\n    if ((rc = ledger_inc_nonce(ctx->ledger, tx->from)) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_inc_nonce failed\");\n        goto exit;\n    }\n    if ((rc = ledger_debit(ctx->ledger, tx->from,\n                           tx->value + (tx->gas_price * tx->gas_limit))) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_debit failed\");\n        goto exit;\n    }\n    if (tx->value > 0 && tx->to[0] != '\\0') {\n        if ((rc = ledger_credit(ctx->ledger, tx->to, tx->value)) != 0) {\n            log_error(\"tp_apply: ledger_credit failed\");\n            goto exit;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Domain-specific: NFT state transitions, governance votes, etc. */\n    switch (tx->type) {\n        case TX_MINT_NFT:\n            rc = artifact_state_on_mint(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.mint);\n            break;\n        case TX_TRANSFER_NFT:\n            rc = artifact_state_on_transfer(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.transfer);\n            break;\n        case TX_GOVERNANCE_VOTE:\n            rc = governance_apply_vote(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.vote);\n            break;\n        case TX_STATE_MACHINE_EVOLUTION:\n            rc = artifact_state_transition(ctx->ledger, &tx->payload.evolve);\n            break;\n        default:\n            /* For payment-only transactions nothing else to do */\n            break;\n    }\n\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_apply: domain mutation failed (rc=%d)\", rc);\n        /* TODO: roll-back ledger_write via undo-log once implemented */\n    }\n\nexit:\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&ctx->mtx);\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Event-Bus integration                                                    */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void tp_emit_event(txp_ctx_t *ctx, const tx_t *tx)\n{\n    event_t ev = {\n        .topic = \"ledger.tx.applied\",\n        .ts_unix = (uint64_t)time(NULL)\n    };\n    snprintf(ev.key, sizeof(ev.key), \"%s\", tx->tx_id);\n\n    /* Copy raw transaction as payload; consumer services know how to decode */\n    ev.payload     = tx->raw;\n    ev.payload_len = tx->raw_len;\n\n    if (event_bus_publish(ctx->bus, &ev) != 0) {\n        log_error(\"tp_emit_event: failed to publish event for tx %s\", tx->tx_id);\n    }\n}\n\n/*
